Confounders = ESTIMATE.TumorPurity + CIN (HRD unavailable in CPTAC CLI). Data built by `scripts/cptac/{fetch,ingest}_cptac2*`; resolves the MH-31/MH-33 "no independent breast-miRNA cohort" caveat. A slope-free **L1b `protein_resid`** layer was added to MH-33's TCGA-105 run too (also null there). **Batch (MH-40, `--batch-kind plex`):** adjusting for the literal 15-plex TMT batch (PDC000120) *strengthens* the validation — protein-anticorr FDR-neg genes **53→56**, translational `protein_resid` **11→29** (MS-batch de-noising), so the independent-cohort protein footprint is not a batch artifact; `cptac_validation/prospective/batch_plex/`. |
| MH-35 | **The genuine translational-repression survivors are driven by literature-validated miRNA arms, are subtype-structured, and recover the basal miR-17~92→PTEN hub at the protein layer.** Single-edge resolution of the 44 MH-34 `protein_resid` survivors (per candidate high-evidence arm: partial Spearman of the arm's *own* expression vs the gene's protein-beyond-mRNA residual, adj purity+CIN): **17/23 testable survivors have an FDR driver arm, and ALL 17 are literature-validated edges** (15 miRTarBase functional-MTI, 2 breast-PMID-specific) — **none novel**. Headline: **PTEN's residual is carried by the miR-17~92 cluster** — miR-17-5p (ρ=−0.34, q=0.008), miR-19a-3p (−0.30, **breast-validated**), miR-18a-5p (−0.30), miR-20a-5p (−0.28) — i.e. exactly the subproject's basal hub family (MH-18/20/21), now showing **protein-level** repression in an independent cohort. Other validated drivers: KRT8←miR-186-5p, PEBP1(RKIP)←miR-155-5p, DDAH1←miR-210-3p, BMP1←miR-194-5p, SLPI←miR-146a-5p, ZYX←miR-16-5p, HMGN2←miR-23a-3p. **Subtype-structured** (8 Basal / 7 LumA / 2 LumB): Basal carries a tumor-suppressor/cytoskeletal node (**PTEN**, KRT8, ZYX, HMGN2, SLPI, BMP1, MINPP1, MTCH2); LumA carries metabolic/immune (DDAH1, PEBP1, ARHGDIA, NUPR1, EPHB2, KRT5). PTEN←miR-17-5p is most negative in Basal/LumB (ρ −0.37/−0.36) vs LumA (−0.14). | **S** (driver arms + FDR) / P (subtype, small per-stratum n) | `cptac_validation/prospective/resid_survivors/{survivor_drivers.tsv,survivor_arm_candidates.tsv,survivor_subtype.tsv}` | Module `cptac_resid_survivors.py`. The single-edge test answers the "which miRNA / just an edge?" question for the translational layer; the miR-17~92→PTEN protein-residual hit is the strongest external corroboration of the basal-hub thesis. **Caveats:** per-PAM50 n is small (Basal ≈16) so subtype p-values are individually NS — `top_pam50` is the descriptive strongest-coupling lineage, the FDR claim is the cohort-level driver. "Literature-validated" = miRTarBase functional-MTI / breast PMID (the repo's curated layer), not a fresh per-edge search. **Batch (MH-40, `--batch-kind plex`):** all **17/17 FDR driver arms hold** under the literal 15-plex TMT batch adjustment (none drop); `cptac_validation/prospective/resid_survivors/batch_plex/`. |
| MH-36 | **Orphan (non-miRTarBase) edges DO surface novel protein-level candidate edges in CPTAC — a 93-edge translational candidate queue, with the method self-validated by recovering miR-29→collagen de novo.** Screening **20,663 orphan edges** (TargetScan `ts_weight≥0.25` OR ENCORI `clipExpNum≥2`, absent from miRTarBase functional-MTI) over the Hallmark universe against CPTAC protein (cov-residualized partial Spearman; prospective primary + TCGA-105 replication direction): **15,512 testable → 539 protein-anti-correlation candidates, 182 translational (`protein_resid`) candidates, and 93 translational AND TCGA-replicated** (the strongest tier). **76/93 are fully uncurated** (no miRTarBase row at all) and **29/93 carry both priors** (sequence + CLIP). **Method validation:** the **miR-29 family→ECM/collagen axis emerges de novo** — miR-29a-3p→COL11A1 (ts_weight 0.80, clipExpNum 15), →COL6A2, →BMP1; miR-29b/c→COL11A1 — a textbook miRNA-ECM axis uncurated at these specific targets. Other dual-prior hits: miR-17-5p→DPYSL2/NEDD4L (hub family, new targets), miR-15/16→FKBP1A/ARHGDIA/AP2B1, miR-30e→MINPP1/VIM, miR-150-5p→SPARC, miR-301a-3p→ARHGEF12. | P/H (nominations) | `cptac_validation/orphan_discovery/{orphan_candidates.tsv,orphan_edges_tested.tsv.gz,method_manifest.json}` | Module `cptac_orphan_discovery.py`. Protein-layer analogue of MH-26's `mirtar_gap_coupling_queue`, but the **`protein_resid` (translational, beyond-mRNA) + two-cohort** filters make these stronger than the prior mRNA-only orphan hits. **These are wet-lab NOMINATIONS (CLIP/luciferase), NOT validated edges:** protein anti-correlation ≠ direct edge — co-expression confound persists (esp. ECM/collagen genes co-vary with stroma despite purity+CIN adjustment), and seed-family arms (miR-29a/b/c) cannot be separated. The de-novo recovery of miR-29→collagen is the credibility anchor; the 76 fully-uncurated dual-prior translational hits are the discovery surface. **Batch (MH-40, `--batch-kind plex`):** under the literal 15-plex TMT batch the candidate *counts* grow (all-candidate transl+TCGA-replicated 93→122; the **fully-uncurated** "no-miRTarBase-studies" subset 76→105) but **membership churns ~36%**: of the 76 fully-uncurated strongest-tier edges only **49 persist**, 27 drop, 56 new. The **miR-29→collagen anchor persists/expands**; several drops are the **MH-39 composition artifacts** (miR-142-3p→NCK2/STAM2/TPM4, miR-103a→VCL) — plex partly captures composition/accrual. The **prediction-only tier** (TS-only, no CLIP, no studies — the MH-39 set) is cleaner: translational+replicated 21→29 with **16/21 persisting**, and the 5 drops are *exactly* the MH-39 composition artifacts (miR-103a→VCL, miR-142-3p→NCK2/STAM2/TPM4) while the PMM1 (miR-15a/16/195)/PPM1A (miR-19a/b) core holds — MS-batch independently reproduces the composition story. The TS-only oncomiR-family **protein**-tier edges **miR-20a/93→MMP2** also survive (ρ −0.46/−0.40 → −0.38/−0.41, q sig, replicated). **Caveat:** the count growth is partly anticonservative — `_spearman_p` uses n−2 dof and does *not* deduct the 15 plex dummies, so trust the persistors + named anchors over the raw count. `cptac_validation/orphan_discovery/batch_plex/`. |

| MH-37 | **The miRNA→target anti-correlation replicates in an independent breast cohort of new patients (Buffa 2011).** Buffa (GEO GSE22216 miRNA + GSE22219 mRNA, **207 paired primary breast tumours, no TCGA overlap**) reproduces the TCGA edge coupling: across 3,613 shared HE edges the **Buffa-vs-TCGA partial-ρ concordance is +0.32** (Spearman) and **67% of TCGA neg-sig repressive edges keep a negative sign in Buffa** (both adjusted for an E2F/G2M proliferation proxy + ER status; Buffa lacks purity/HRD). Concordance is stable when ER is added (+0.335→+0.319), i.e. not a subtype-composition artifact. Independently, **HE edges couple more than TargetScan-orphan edges** (Buffa neg-sig frac 0.052 vs 0.032), reproducing the MH-23 HE>orphan gap in a second cohort. | S/R | `output/buffa_validation/{he_edge_coupling_buffa_vs_tcga,orphan_edge_coupling_buffa}.tsv`, `figures/buffa_vs_tcga_replication.png` | **Independent-patient leg** the same-patient CPTAC protein layer (MH-34) cannot give. Caveats: n=207, Illumina **microarray** miRNA (legacy bare arm names → mapped by bare stem, guide-arm-accurate, passenger-arm edges approximate), proliferation+ER adjustment only; **only 13% of TCGA neg-sig edges reach FDR-significance in Buffa** (smaller n, different platform) — so the replication headline is **sign-concordance / ρ-concordance, not significance count**. **Critical method note:** the GEO title index (`BCmicroRNA N` vs `BCmRNA N`) is NOT the patient pairing (scrambles to ρ≈0, sign-concord 0.50); samples matched by clinical fingerprint (age/size/nodes/ER/grade/DRFS), 207/210 unique. |

| MH-38 | **⛔ RETRACTED (MH-114, 2026-07-12; retraction applied here 2026-07-17 — it had been recorded ONLY inside MH-114's row for 5 days while this row still read S/R and the talk still cited it).** **The screen that produced these 594 candidates had NO cell-composition block** — `analyses/cptac/cptac_orphan_discovery.py` built `purity+cin` straight off the clinical frame and never called `_covariates`. Re-run composition-adjusted: **594 → 23 candidates** (kept 20, lost 574, 3 new). **The fixed 23-row list contains ZERO collagen and ZERO ECM edges — the entire miR-29→ECM axis that WAS this claim's headline (34 of the 108) is gone from the nomination list.** This is what MH-112 predicts (90.5% of `composition_explained` edges are OPPOSITE-compartment: miRNA epithelial, collagen stromal ⇒ bulk-mixing arithmetic) and what MH-114's shuffled null proved (site-free non-edges reproduce the real compartment gradient exactly, −0.1290 vs −0.1272). ⚠ **`output/buffa_validation/*` is still dated 2026-06-26 and has NOT been re-run**, so the "108" below is computed from the retracted 594-row input. **Do not cite 108.** Corroborated independently and 16 days EARLY by **MH-55** (`dcis_orphan_rigor`, 2026-06-26): ECM/collagen orphans preferentially attenuate under composition adjustment (neg 84%→62%, median ρ halved) — and note the fixed 23-list does **not** contain MH-55's "surviving core" (let-7d→COL1A2, miR-29a→COL1A1, miR-128→FBLN2) either. **✅ RE-RUN 2026-07-17 — MEASURED, NOT EXPECTED: 108 → 3.** `triple_validated` **108 → 3**; **ECM/collagen among triple-validated 30 → 0** (all rows: 64 → 0). The 3 survivors are all miRGeneDB **guide** arms: **miR-195-5p→PSMD7** (buffa ρ=−0.20, cptac −0.40, tcga −0.06), **miR-181b-5p→IRS2** (−0.02/−0.45/−0.13), **miR-22-3p→STK39** (−0.01/−0.39/−0.32); only **1** is neg-sig in Buffa. Funnel: 23 candidates → 20 genuine → 11 testable in Buffa → 3 also CPTAC-sig + TCGA-replicated → 3 same-sign (100%). **The pre-registered expectation ("single digits, possibly zero ECM") was CORRECT.** ⚠ **THE RE-RUN REQUIRED A CODE FIX — the module had been DEAD, not merely stale:** `GEO_DIR` was built as `Path(__file__).parent.parent/"data"`, correct when the module lived at the subproject top level, but it was later moved into `eval/` — which added a directory level and pointed it at the non-existent `mirna_hallmark/data/`. Every input raised `FileNotFoundError`; the data was at the repo root all along. Fixed to `C.REPO_ROOT / "data" / ...` (config is the single source of truth for paths; every other path in the module already used `C.*`). **CONTROL — the HE-edge legs are untouched and reproduce exactly** (concordance **+0.319**, sign-concordance **0.593**, TCGA neg-sig same-sign **0.673**, neg-sig-in-Buffa **0.128**, HE 0.052 vs orphan 0.032), confirming the fix perturbed only the orphan lane. Superseded claim retained verbatim below for provenance. ⟶ ORIGINAL CLAIM (RETRACTED): **Genuine orphan edges — TargetScan/ENCORI-predicted, completely absent from miRTarBase — validate across THREE independent readouts (TCGA mRNA · CPTAC protein · Buffa mRNA).** Of 594 CPTAC orphan candidates, **492 are genuine orphans** (`mirtar_any==False`, no miRTarBase study at all). Of those that were CPTAC-protein-significant AND TCGA-replicated and testable in the independent Buffa cohort (n=127), **108 (85%) keep a negative sign in Buffa** — far above the 50% chance rate — giving **108 triple-cohort-validated orphan edges** (47 reach FDR-significance in Buffa). **All 108 are miRGeneDB high-confidence guide arms** (37 miRNAs, 67 genes), i.e. legitimate miRNAs (in the curated ~65%) acting on targets uncurated by miRTarBase. The set is led by the **miR-29 family → ECM/collagen** axis (29 edges: COL1A2/COL5A1/COL5A3/COL6A2/COL6A3/COL11A1, FSTL1, BMP1, LOXL2, CALU) — 34/108 are ECM targets — plus miR-205→TXNRD1, miR-150→SPARC, miR-15/16→AHNAK/FERMT2. | **⛔ RETRACTED** (was S/R — the tag a reader trusts; downgraded 2026-07-17) | `output/buffa_validation/orphan_triple_cohort_validation.tsv` | Extends MH-26/MH-37 and the CPTAC `cptac_orphan_discovery`. These are **curation-gap edges with the strongest possible cross-cohort support short of luciferase** — real miRNAs, real targets, coherent repression in 3 independent datasets/2 layers, but still **nominations** (coupling ≠ direct binding; miR-29→collagen is established R, the rest a prioritised CLIP/luciferase queue). 85% Buffa same-sign >> the 67% HE-edge rate (MH-37) because these were pre-selected for 2-cohort coupling. **All-guide is partly a measurement artifact, NOT proof non-miRGeneDB arms fail:** the pool had 26 non-guide genuine orphans (16 true double-orphans not in miRGeneDB, 8 passenger, 2 ambiguous); none triple-validated, but **14/16 double-orphans are not even probed by the Buffa Illumina v1 array** (rare/non-canonical arms), and the 2 testable (miR-520b→WFS1/AKT1) were not TCGA-replicated and went positive in Buffa. So the funnel is structurally biased toward guide arms (cross-cohort coupling + array coverage both favour real miRNAs). Reinforces miRGeneDB-as-filter (cf. MH-32) but a fair double-orphan test needs a cohort that *measures* them (miRNA-seq, not microarray). |

| MH-39 | **Cell-composition adjustment splits the prediction-only orphan tier into a cell-intrinsic core and a stromal/immune-lineage artifact (pilot).** Re-testing the 24 prediction-only (TargetScan-sequence-only, no CLIP, no miRTarBase) translational+TCGA-replicated orphans (MH-36) after adding **PAM50 + CAF / endothelial / immune marker composite scores** to the residualization (base purity+CIN prospective / CPE+HRD tcga105): **17/24 `protein_resid` survivors hold** in prospective (q<0.1), **18/21 stay negative** in tcga105. The **7 drops are exactly the composition-loaded targets**: VCL (CAF ρ=0.79, endo 0.54), TPM4 (CAF 0.67), HOOK3/RNH1 (CAF ~0.49), and the **miR-142-3p cluster WASL/STAM2** (load *negatively* on immune, −0.40/−0.32 — miR-142 is a hematopoietic-lineage arm, so the "edge" was immune-content co-variation). The robust core is **metabolic/composition-neutral**: the **miR-15/16/195→PMM1** convergence (q≈0.002, CAF-neutral after adjustment), **miR-19a/b-3p→PPM1A** (CAF ρ=0.06), miR-107→VCAN (survives despite stromal context), let-7g→CERCAM, miR-532→PURA, miR-101→RAB1A. | P/H (nominations) | `cptac_validation/orphan_discovery/confound_pilot/{pilot_edge_confound_comparison,pilot_marker_loadings}.tsv` | Module `cptac_orphan_confound_pilot.py` (`--use-estimate` toggles the fraction source). **Pilot scope** — 24-edge prediction-only tier only. **Robust to the deconvolution method:** repeating with **true ESTIMATE ssGSEA fractions** (ImmuneScore+StromalScore, R `estimate`, run per cohort on its own RNA; StromalScore folds CAF+endothelium, endothelial marker kept separately) gives **16/24** prospective survivors (vs 17 with marker composites) and **18/21** tcga105 — same robust core (PMM1, PPM1A, VCAN, CERCAM), same drops (VCL, TPM4, miR-142-3p cluster). Tightens MH-36: the strongest discovery tier is real but ~29% is cell-composition; **PMM1/PPM1A are the composition-robust de-novo nominations**, method-independent. Next: extend the same confounder set to the full 93/594 orphan candidates and to the curated `protein_resid` survivors (MH-35). **Widest-confounder evidence table** (`cptac_orphan_evidence_table.py`) adds **target-gene CN** (the missing covariate) to the full stack (base + PAM50 + ESTIMATE Immune/Stromal + endo + target CN), across TCGA + both CPTAC RNA and CPTAC protein/`resid`: the core (miR-195/16/15a→PMM1, PPM1A, NCK2, KIF1B, VCAN) **survives even with target CN partialled**; the PMM1 edges are mRNA-flat / protein-strong = translational. **Batch (MH-40, `--batch-kind plex`, plex added to base cov):** with composition on top of base+TMT-plex, prospective resid survivors **24→13** and tcga105 **21→20**; the same composition artifacts drop (VCL/WASL/STAM2/TPM4/HOOK3) and the PMM1/PPM1A core holds — the composition story is unchanged by MS-batch adjustment; `confound_pilot/batch_plex/`. |

| MH-40 | **The translational survivor couplings are TARGET-SPECIFIC, not a global anti-correlation artifact — each driver arm anti-correlates with its cognate protein but NOT with the unrelated proteome.** Decoy/placebo control on the MH-35 survivor drivers (prospective): for each driver arm, the cognate `protein_resid` coupling is compared against the *same* arm's coupling to every Hallmark-universe protein it does **not** target (miRTarBase any-evidence ∪ TargetScan seed ∪ ENCORI CLIP all excluded; ≥50 decoys; purity+CIN-residualized partial Spearman, same machinery as MH-35/36). **19 edges testable (13 FDR drivers). The cognate couplings (median ρ=−0.33) sit ~16× more negative than the unrelated decoy null (grand decoy median ρ=−0.021), which is centred essentially at zero — unrelated proteins are NOT broadly negative.** Per-arm, **9/13 FDR drivers are specific** (cognate in the decoy left tail, empirical p<0.05: KRT8←miR-186-5p, DDAH1←miR-210-3p, BMP1←miR-194-5p, PEBP1←miR-155-5p, ARHGDIA←miR-151a-5p, HMGN2←miR-23a-3p, MINPP1←miR-30b-5p, SLPI←miR-146a-5p, MTCH2←miR-135b-5p) and **12/13 at p<0.10**. The 2 borderline cases are exactly the broad proliferation oncomiRs whose decoy nulls are the most-negative of any arm — **PTEN←miR-17-5p (emp p=0.062; decoy median −0.060)** and ZYX←miR-16-5p (0.060) — i.e. the control correctly *discounts* arms that are genuinely somewhat globally anti-correlated, exactly as a specificity test should. **Batch-confounder control (literal TMT plex):** adding the **literal prospective TMT-plex dummies** (15 plexes; PDC study PDC000120, fetched by `scripts/cptac/fetch_cptac_brca_tmt_plex.py`; the canonical proteomics batch) to the purity+CIN residualization leaves **8/9 specific edges plex-robust** — only **MTCH2←miR-135b-5p** drops (cognate ρ −0.26→−0.21, emp p 0.034→0.094). Notably **HMGN2←miR-23a-3p survives the literal plex** (it had dropped under the coarser enrolling-**site** proxy, which over-aggregates 6 site dummies and knocked out 7/9→ HMGN2+MTCH2). So the precise batch adjustment is *cleaner* than the proxy and the survivor core (KRT8, MINPP1, DDAH1, ARHGDIA, BMP1, PEBP1, SLPI, HMGN2) is confirmed target-specific even after removing MS-plex structure. | **S** (specificity) | `cptac_validation/prospective/target_specificity/{arm_specificity.tsv,decoy_couplings.tsv.gz,method_manifest.json}` | Module `cptac_target_specificity.py` (batch pass default `--batch-kind plex`; `site`/`none`/`auto` selectable; `arm_specificity.tsv` carries `*_batch` + `batch_robust` columns). Batch helpers shared via `mirna_hallmark/cptac_batch.py` and **propagated to MH-34/35/36/39** (`--batch-kind plex` writes a `batch_plex/` subdir per module; prospective = TMT plex PDC000120, tcga105 = iTRAQ `Experiment Order` from the Sample-Mapping xlsx). Directly answers the reviewer worry that the MH-35 protein-residual couplings could be a confound (proliferation / purity / cell-composition / MS batch) that suppresses the whole proteome — they are not: the negativity is concentrated on the cognate target and survives the literal plex. Complements MH-39 (composition covariates on the *orphan* tier) by attacking the confound from the **target-selectivity** side on the *curated* survivors. **Caveats:** seed-family-shared targets only partly removed (TargetScan-seed exclusion); the decoy-vs-0 Wilcoxon is over-powered (the *magnitude* gap is the read, not its p); protein anti-correlation still ≠ direct binding (MH-35 ceiling). PTEN/miR-17-5p remains a real edge (literature + MH-35 FDR) whose specificity is merely diluted by the cluster's broad oncomiR action. |
| MH-41 | **The cohort-wide signed-NMF gain backbone is a flat, pan-subtype `miR-21/26a/let-7`-driven program led by RASGRP1/MSH2/CCL20/VHL/HPGD — *not* the LIFR/ABCE1/IGFBP5 factor the prior report named (a stale-fit artifact, now corrected).** The signed (gain+loss) gene-pressure NMF is dominated by **one** factor (S3, mean H-activation ≈2× any other) that is the *most-shared, least-divergent* factor: flat across PAM50 (.27/.27/.30/.15, so its `Her2:KRAS_SIGNALING_UP` label is a thin-margin argmax, not real Her2 specificity), dominant in **725/1096** tumours, **45% of tumours at ≥50% share**. The §2 acquired oncomiR genes (LIFR/ABCE1/IGFBP5; IL12A/PCBP1/TPM1) are *distinct minor* factors (S9 `Basal:MYC`; S2/S8 `ESTROGEN`). **Cause of the prior error:** `nmf_sample_signatures` re-fit its **own** signed NMF independent of `mirna_comovement`'s, and the two fits returned permutation-arbitrary factor indices, so a bare-index read crossed the per-sample dominance table with the loadings/factor_subtype tables and mislabelled the backbone. **Fix:** comovement now persists full `W`/`H`; sample_signatures reuses them — one fit, identical indices+labels everywhere (join by `factor_label`, never F-index). **Unchanged interpretation:** still one shared "everything-up" gain backbone (no organised relief program anywhere; gain-mass .93–.99 across all 12 factors), with the subtype signal living in the **high-KW secondaries** — Basal MYC (S12, .51 Basal, q8e-95) + Basal E2F (S1, .51, q8e-76) = a *proliferation* axis, vs Luminal/Her2 hormone+immune secondaries; universal driver core `miR-26a-5p`+`let-7i-5p` (top-8 of all 12 factors), distinctive arm in the tail. | S (structural) / methods-fix | `tissue_reference/mirna_comovement/nmf_gene_pressure_signed_{W,H,loadings,factor_subtype}.tsv`, `nmf_sample_signatures/{summary_all,per_sample/gene_pressure_signed_cohort}.tsv` | Corrects `LANDSCAPE_REPORT.md §6b` (backbone identity + full 12-factor catalog added). NMF "dominance" on the gain axis is a *universal co-directional acquisition*, **not** discrete patient subtypes — partly methodological (one-signed gain matrix). Factor F-indices remain permutation-arbitrary across rebuilds; the stable handle is `factor_label`. |
| MH-42 | **Within-pathway NMF resolves sub-modules inside a single Hallmark program — and the CANONICAL z-spine pressure (not abundance) is the resolving lens, with a competitive-restructuring view (miR-21 gains / miR-29 loses grip) from a second edge-level family.** Restricting the matrix to one set's genes (33/50 sets ≥40 genes; per-set elbow k) surfaces structure the cross-set NMF averages away, across **two families**. **Family A (gene×sample pressure, 5 flavours × cohort+4 PAM50):** with per-set auto-k the chosen k *is* the sub-structure signal — the **z-spine (`softmax_z_logrpm`, the canonical coupling pressure) and hybrid resolve most (elbow k≈5, ~2.6–2.9 effective sub-modules)** while **abundance (no-z) collapses to ~one module (k=2, eff 1.7)** because the few high-abundance arms own the set's pressure; acquired/signed are intermediate (k≈4). *This corrects the earlier fixed-k=4 prototype reading that "abundance resolves" — under a principled elbow the no-z abundance has the LEAST resolvable structure, vindicating inclusion of the z-spine/hybrid flavours.* Sub-modules are sharp and subtype-split: **EMT (spine)** → inflammatory-contractile MYLK/INHBA/VEGFA/IL6 (Basal, q1e-109) · **miR-29a/b/c→collagen** COL4A1/SPARC/FBN1/COL5A2 (pan-subtype, KW-non-sig — recovers the orphan-discovery miR-29→ECM axis MH-36/38 AND is correctly flagged stromal-composition per MH-39) · miR-30/182 mesenchymal-marker; **E2F (spine)** → Basal replication RAD21/STMN1 (miR-92a/197) vs LumA mitotic RRM2/TOP2A (let-7) vs splicing/checkpoint. **Within-subtype** re-fit adds structure for some programs (APOPTOSIS eff 1.5 cohort → 3.2 inside Her2; HYPOXIA → 3.6 inside LumB). **Family B (edge-level miRNA×gene lenses — different matrix, no sample axis): aggregate / share / grip_change.** The **share** lens shows the miR-29 family *owns* EMT's collagen budget; the **grip_change** lens (Δshare tumor−gtex) gives a clean restructuring story — in EMT **miR-21/miR-17-3p GAIN grip** (RHOB/TGFBI/COL4A1/VEGFA/TIMP3) while **miR-29a/b/c LOSE grip** on collagen (FBN1/COL4A1/COL5A2/SPARC), and in E2F miR-21/449a/b gain grip (MSH2/CDC25A/MYC/CHEK1) vs miR-7/1246 losing it — control handed from suppressor families to the oncomiR. | P (prototype) / S (sub-module + grip recovery) | `tissue_reference/within_pathway_nmf/{factor_summary,set_dominance,lens_factors,per_sample.tsv.gz}.tsv` + `figures/` | Module `within_pathway_nmf.py` (`--sets`, `--flavours`, `--kmax`, `--min-genes`, `--no-within-subtype`); wired into `run_tissue_reference_full.sh` step 12. | MH-44 | **The pressure *construction* changes realized coupling only through the softmax SHARE — and its effect is resolution-dependent: it weakens single-edge coupling but improves the gene aggregate.** Head-to-head partial-Spearman coupling (CPE+HRD+target-CN, BH-FDR per predictor, ungated) under each predictor construction vs a raw-abundance baseline. **Within-arm transforms are rank-equivalent to abundance, as predicted:** at the **edge** level (5,101 edges, abundance baseline = **1,487** neg-sig) `z` reproduces abundance (sign-concordance **0.97**, ρ-rankcorr 0.96, recovers **0.95** of neg-sig) — confirming z is an affine (monotone) transform of abundance and the static edge weight is a sample-constant scalar, so neither moves a rank-coupling. **The softmax share is the sole differentiator:** share-alone recovers only **0.51** of the abundance neg-sig and its overall median partial-ρ flips *positive* (+0.008) — the co-regulator normalization injects other arms' dynamics and **weakens** single-edge coupling; re-adding level/dynamics climbs back (softmax_logrpm 0.68, softmax_z 0.86, spine softmax_z_logrpm 0.82 recovery, 1,310 neg-sig). **So raw abundance is the most sensitive *edge* predictor — vindicating the pipeline's use of abundance at edge/module resolution.** At the **gene** level (1,408 genes, abundance-sum baseline = **399** neg-sig) the picture inverts: pure share is **degenerate** (per-gene softmax sums to ≈1 each sample → near-constant → ρ-rankcorr **−0.16**, only 166 neg-sig), but the *full* pressure **beats the naive abundance-sum** — `softmax_z` **435** and spine `softmax_z_logrpm` **430** neg-sig (**+8%**) with a deeper median-ρ-among-negatives — i.e. share×dynamics×level is a better way to fold many regulators into one dose than summing raw abundances. **Net: pressure ≠ abundance, the difference is entirely the share term, and whether it helps depends on resolution (hurts one edge, helps the gene aggregate).** | **S** (methods/sensitivity) | `coupling_predictor_comparison/{edge_coupling_by_predictor,gene_coupling_by_predictor}.tsv.gz`, `predictor_comparison_summary.tsv` | Module `coupling_predictor_comparison.py` (review-driven; | MH-45 | **⛔ THE HEADLINE IS RETRACTED BY MH-154 — "survives a permutation null" IS NOT PROTECTION HERE: this permutation null IS the theoretical null (σ₀=0.0309 vs 0.0311, μ₀=+0.0001, R²=0.9997), because a permutation destroys the unmodelled population structure that inflates the real null. The edge rung's 1,013 BY-sig (33.3% of 3,040) sits INSIDE the 35.3% rate at which IMPOSSIBLE edges clear the same gate under the same C (MH-123). BY corrects dependence, not scale. The seed-family/BY machinery below is correctly implemented and the justification stands — the defect is the NULL they sit on. Read the per-edge counts as UNCALIBRATED; the calibration standard is `eval/site_free_null.py`.** Original claim: **Every coupling claim survives a permutation null AND a dependence-robust FDR at all five resolutions — and the seed-family collapse that makes the FDR dependence-aware is empirically justified, deflating the edge "discovery" count to its honest family size.** Freedman–Lane rank-residual permutation (one shared sample permutation per draw, so seed-family correlation is preserved in the null; a smooth Gaussian-tail `p_z` from the null moments feeds the FDR so the discrete 1/(B+1) floor never caps it, with `p_perm` kept as the honest exceedance check), applied at **edge / gene / target-set / program / universe** (n≈1041 tumour, Normal-like excluded; ungated; CPE+HRD+TCGA-batch; 2000 perms). **Results:** edge 3,040 tests → 1,795 neg, **1,208 BH / 1,013 BY** neg-sig, collapsing to **108 / 577 significant seed families**; gene 1,420 → 464 BH / **373 BY**; target-set 152 → 70 / **57**, 49/278 families; program 50 → 37 / **34 BY**; **universe 37 coupled programs vs a permutation count-null of 2.5 (p95=13), p=0.0015.** **Seed-family dependence is measured, not assumed** (`seed_family_justification`): within-seed-family arm pairs co-vary ≈2× random pairs (median Spearman 0.375 vs 0.175, Mann–Whitney p≈4.3e-14; shared-target Jaccard 0.053 vs 0.0), and the multi-arm hubs (miR-302/372/373/520, miR-320, miR-200) are **20–37% redundant** (Nyholt effective-test count) — so plain BH over edges is anti-conservative and the Simes family collapse is warranted; the effect is localized (≈9.5% aggregate over multi-arm families, ~3% globally since most seed families are singletons). | **S** (inference rigor) | `coupling_permutation/{manifest.json, coupling_{edge,gene,target_set,program}.tsv.gz}`, `coupling_inference/seed_family_justification/{summary.json, per_family_redundancy.tsv}` | Modules `coupling_inference.py` (seed-family map, Benjamini–Yekutieli, Simes family FDR, min-stat family FWER, joint permutation), `coupling_permutation.py` (all-rung runner), `seed_family_justification.py`. **Two senses of "family" both handled** — statistical testing-family (BH/BY scope) and biological miRNA seed-family (Simes collapse). **Caveats:** the per-gene target-CN ladder rung stays in the parametric path (a row-specific covariate breaks the shared-permutation vectorization); Per arm the acquired healthy→tumour direction is read **same-modality** from the GTEx-anchored *abundance* trajectory (`gtex_to_tumor_pct`; GTEx-absent + `tumor_induced` → +1, per the floor-0 healthy-anchor policy — **not** the pressure dHT, which folds in edge weights the array can't see); the magnitude/significance is the **same-platform** DCIS-vs-IBC Mann–Whitney + rank-biserial; TCGA enters for **direction only** (rank). v16→modern arm bridge = 663 arms (155 exact, 384 abundance-guide, 124 abundance-star; `bridge_method` recorded per arm). **Per-arm BH is underpowered (n_ibc=14; min q=0.23; 0 BH-sig; nominal p<0.05 = 15 divergent + 7 late), so the headline is the POWERED set-level test:** acquired **GAINERS (n=351)** show median oriented rank-biserial **−0.077**, only **34.8%** still moving toward the tumour state, signed-rank **p=4.0e-6** → **no net further gain → pre-invasive establishment**; acquired **LOSERS (n=57)** median **+0.077**, **82.5%** still moving, **p=6.9e-5** → **loss continues at invasion.** The strongest individual DCIS→IBC shifts are **all downward** and are myoepithelial / stromal / immune arms — **miR-133b (rb −0.67), miR-338-3p, miR-126-3p, miR-223-3p, miR-145-3p/-5p, miR-497-5p, miR-140-3p** — exactly the **myoepithelial-layer loss that histologically defines invasion**. | MH-47 | **The pressure construction is validated out-of-sample and robust to its hand-tuned constants — and the promiscuity denominator is re-justified on the *attribution* axis after the held-out showed it is coupling-neutral.** **(a) Held-out (5-fold patient CV, 8 constructions, n=1041):** the candidate ranking reproduces *perfectly* out-of-sample (train→test rank-ρ **1.0**, selection stability **1.0**) → the spine construction is **not overfit** to in-sample coupling (the circularity worry is refuted) — but it is **not coupling-maximal** either: bare `softmax_z_logrpm|none` is best (test mean partial-ρ −0.0265) and the spine `…|evidence_mass` is **3rd** (−0.0256), a consistent but negligible 0.0009 gap (~1.5% of strong-neg genes). **(b) Constant sensitivity** (±25% log-normal jitter of all evidence weights + systematic ±50% per weight + AGO-weight grid): the coupling headline is **robust** — `n_strong_neg` stays within **0.90–1.01×** baseline (CV 0.06), mean-ρ CV 0.01. **(c) Attribution sweep (1,408 genes):** the denominator is coupling-neutral **and specificity-invariant** (spec rank-ρ exactly **1.0** — `D(m)` cancels in the within-arm ratio) but **materially reshapes cross-arm attribution** — it re-assigns a gene's dominant regulator for **~11%** of genes (dominant-arm concordance 0.89 vs `none`) and de-concentrates the hub structure (Gini **0.570→0.538**, dominance spread from **~43→~56** arms). **Net:** the construction generalizes (non-circular); the denominator earns its place as *promiscuity control on attribution*, not as a coupling lever, and `evidence_mass` is a principled middle (a pure `degree` penalty de-concentrates marginally more), not a uniquely optimal pick. | **S** (methods/validation) | `held_out_tuning/{per_candidate,per_fold,summary.json}`, `pressure_constant_sensitivity/{sensitivity_scores.tsv,summary.json}`, `denominator_attribution_sweep/{attribution_by_denominator.tsv,summary.json}` | Modules `held_out_tuning.py`, `pressure_constant_sensitivity.py`, `denominator_attribution_sweep.py`. **Corrects** the §3.3 doc rationale (was "sharpest/most stable coupling, Basal ≥42/50" — a coupling claim the held-out refutes; the value is on attribution). **Caveats:** coupling metric is aggregate gene-level mean partial-ρ (CPE+HRD+batch), ungated; held-out uses 5 random folds, seed 0. |

| id | hypothesis |
|----|------------|
| MH-H1 | Strata with high regulatory-miRNA expression for a Hallmark show lower target-gene expression *within stratum* (stratum-resolved interaction, not just cohort). |
| MH-H2 | miRNA-locus loss co-occurs with target-program up-regulation for specific high-evidence families (loss-of-repression). |
| MH-H3 | RISC capacity (AGO gate) modifies the pressure↔expression slope more strongly in immune-cold (Thornsson) strata. |
| MH-103 | **The CPTAC protein layer CONFIRMS the mRNA-mediated model and BOUNDS the translational residual below a 101-patient cohort's resolution: miRNA dose adds NOTHING to protein beyond the gene's own transcript in 16/17 genes — and the prior positive evidence for a `βᵗ` latent was a LEAK.** The proposed protein channel's centrepiece — `βᵗ`, a per-seed-family *translational* repression latent that RNA-seq is structurally blind to — is **NOT SUPPORTED at n=101, in ANY framing.** **The leak:** the earlier evidence (OOF 9/9 positive; PTEN z=3.1; PEBP1 z=3.6) built the target `protein_resid = P − a·M` with the mediator slope **`a` fitted on ALL samples**, then OOF-ed `X → protein_resid` — so the mediator saw the test folds. **Fit `a` INSIDE the fold and the signal disappears.** **Leak-free, four independent framings agree:** (a) ridge, all ~60 families, λ swept with best-λ chosen *on the test metric* (deliberately optimistic) → median incremental OOF gain **+0.001**; (b) **NNLS — the model's OWN non-negative estimator, correctly regularized for p≈63>n=101 — all families → z>2 in 0/10 genes**; (c) NNLS restricted to the **top-3 families by TCGA β** (a strong NON-circular prior: different cohort AND different layer; K chosen post-hoc ⇒ optimistic) → z>2 in 3/10 (BCL2 3.62 · CDKN1A 2.30 · PDCD4 2.23), tiny effects d=0.01–0.05; (d) **the AGGREGATE frame — the MAXIMUM-POWER test and the one MH-34 actually used: predictor = `X_cptac·β_TCGA`, weights FIXED from another cohort and layer, ZERO parameters fitted in CPTAC ⇒ 1 df instead of 60** → **BH q<0.10 in 1/17 genes: BCL2 alone** (d=+0.028, q≈0.085, itself marginal). **PTEN — cited as the headline throughout the plan — is DEAD in every framing (aggregate d=−0.006, p=0.82).** ⇒ the negative is **NOT a leak, NOT an estimator choice, and NOT a p>n artifact — it is the TRUE EFFECT SIZE meeting the cohort size.** **Fully coherent with our own prior biology: MH-34 established repression is predominantly mRNA-mediated with a translational residual in 11/1132 ≈ 1% of genes** — on a 17-gene panel that predicts ~0.2 hits and we found 1. **There was never a `βᵗ` FIELD to fit; the plan mistook a ~1%-prevalence phenomenon for a latent dimension. MH-34 ran on **1,132 genes → 11–29 hits ≈ 1–2%**; on 17 genes that predicts **0.2–0.4** hits and we see 0–1 — **quantitatively consistent**. ⚠ An earlier wording ("miRNA adds NOTHING to protein beyond the transcript") **over-claimed and is corrected**: the translational component is **real, weak and widespread-in-DIRECTION**, but **not resolvable per gene at n=101**. **The falsification is of the MODELING OBJECT (a βᵗ latent), not of the biology and not of MH-34.** | **S** (four leak-free out-of-fold tests with permutation nulls + BH-FDR, on real CPTAC miRNA+mRNA+protein, n=101; the max-power aggregate framing is decisive and pre-specified) | `learned/cptac_data.py` (Model-3 assembly; n=101, C=11 matching TCGA, both RNA sources); leak-free incremental-OOF probes (scratchpad); `learned/eval/ood_protein.py` (the aggregate scorer this replicates) | Falsifies the centrepiece of `CPTAC_PROTEIN_CHANNEL_PLAN.md` (see its **§0a**) and retires board **§A③**'s "sole remaining fundamental lever" framing. **Surviving axis (none depend on βᵗ):** `locus_cn_cptac` (BUILT, median r=0.997 vs ASCAT) · V1 δ-transportability · Bar-5 systematic (validates **β** via the `a·β` path — a different, easier claim; MH-83 = 7/7) · the **`a`** propagation slope — see MH-105 correction below. | MH-108-C | **CORRECTION to MH-108: buffering is CAUSAL (confirmed) but it is NOT "2.4× stronger under IV" — that claim was a SELECTION ARTIFACT and is RETRACTED.** The error: I compared **Spearman(complex size, a_OLS) = −0.079 computed on ALL 9,196 genes** against **Spearman(complex size, a_IV) = −0.193 computed on the 4,346 F>10 genes** — **two different samples**. **On the SAME F>10 gene set: OLS ρ = −0.157 (p=1.8e-25) vs IV ρ = −0.136 (p=2.3e-19)** ⇒ **IV is slightly WEAKER, exactly as theory requires** (2SLS is *less efficient* than OLS — it discards the variation in M not explained by Z). The apparent "strengthening" is a **selection effect**: well-instrumented (high-CN-variance) genes simply show a stronger buffering relationship (ρ = −0.157 vs −0.061 over all genes). **WHAT STANDS:** (i) **causal `a_IV` = 0.893 vs `a_OLS` = 0.561** ⇒ mRNA reliability **λ = â_OLS/â_IV = 0.63** (≈37% of observed mRNA variance is measurement noise; independent bound from the two CPTAC RNA pipelines correlating at r=0.81 ⇒ λ ≤ 0.81, an upper bound since they share RNA/library) — **protein tracks mRNA ~1:1 CAUSALLY**; (ii) **buffering IS CAUSAL** — `a_IV` declines MONOTONICALLY with complex size: monomer **0.943** → 2–3 **0.970** → 4–12 **0.849** → 13–30 **0.763** → **≥31: 0.526** (MW p=8.8e-20; Spearman −0.136, p=2.3e-19). **⭐ STRONG INTERNAL VALIDATION of the errors-in-variables model:** the **IV/OLS ratio is CONSTANT across every stratum (1.55–1.68)** — exactly what `â_OLS = a·λ` predicts for a gene-independent λ: IV rescales by 1/λ≈1.6 and leaves the SHAPE intact. And **λ_g varies (IQR 0.46–0.82) but is INDEPENDENT of complex size (Spearman −0.042)** ⇒ attenuation does **not** bias the buffering contrast. | **S** (same-sample OLS-vs-IV comparison on 4,346 F>10 genes; monotone dose-response; the constant IV/OLS ratio across strata is a parameter-free prediction of the attenuation model, confirmed) | `output/learned/cptac_a_causal_iv.tsv` | **Corrects the "2.4× stronger" claim in MH-108.** The causal `a_IV` and the causal-buffering conclusions are UNCHANGED. |
| MH-109 | **THREE results, and the headline is a RETRACTION of my own: (a) protein-complex buffering is SYMMETRIC (deletion 0.53 vs amplification 0.55) — so a miRNA, which only represses, IS pushing in a buffered direction; (b) the MH-106 "mechanistic clincher" (miRNA effect reaches protein only where protein tracks mRNA) is RETRACTED — it does not hold; (c) consequently the protein-CONSEQUENCE filter is delivered as a mechanistically-grounded ANNOTATION, explicitly NOT validated in this cohort.** **(a) BUFFERING IS SYMMETRIC (premise-verification, not a discovery).** Complex buffering is usually attributed to *proteasomal degradation of EXCESS subunit* — which would protect only against OVER-expression and would mean a repressor like a miRNA is pushing in an UNBUFFERED direction. Tested by pooling **826,824 (gene,patient) cells over 8,450 genes** and estimating dP/dM separately in DELETED (CN<1.7, 14.1% of cells) vs AMPLIFIED (CN>2.3, 15.6%) cells, within CORUM complex-size strata: **complex≥31 vs monomer ratio = 0.53 on DELETION and 0.55 on AMPLIFICATION — essentially identical**, with a clean monotone gradient in BOTH directions (deleted 0.394→0.209; amplified 0.487→0.270). ⇒ **complex subunits are damped against BOTH excess and deficit** ⇒ the "a miRNA pushes in an unbuffered direction" objection is REFUTED. (The *concept* of bidirectional buffering is modelled in the literature — Biology Direct 2015; this is an empirical quantification in human cancer tissue, and the check that licenses (c).) **(b) ⛔ RETRACTION of MH-106's "mechanistic clincher".** I claimed "the miRNA effect reaches protein ONLY where protein tracks mRNA" (a_g<0.15 → no transfer; a_g 0.55–0.80 → retention 0.392, p=3e-4) and called it a free internal-consistency check. **It is an ARTIFACT of coarse binning + an unstable ratio-of-medians "retention" statistic.** The **continuous** test: on the 204 genes with a usable CN instrument, Spearman with protein-repression strength is **a_OLS +0.010 (p=0.88) · a_IV +0.032 (p=0.65) · λ (measurement reliability) −0.003 (p=0.97)**; partial a_IV|λ = +0.031 (p=0.66). Over all 490 genes a_OLS gives ρ=+0.103 (p=0.022) — **but instrument strength F (pure CN variance, a nuisance) predicts equally well (ρ=+0.114, p=0.011)** ⇒ **NOT interpretable as biology.** **UNSUPPORTED ≠ DISPROVEN:** the miRNA→protein signal is at the NOISE FLOOR (median ρ = −0.02 against a per-gene SE ≈ 0.10 at n=101), so a *modulation* of it is undetectable by construction. **(c) THE PROTEIN-CONSEQUENCE ANNOTATION (delivered, honestly labelled).** Per-edge score = the TARGET's CAUSAL propagation slope `a_IV` (MH-108). Coverage: **1,768/5,938 HE edges (30%)** have a target with a usable instrument (F>10); of those, **BUFFERED (a_IV<0.40) 157 (8.9%) · partial 669 (37.8%) · CONSEQUENTIAL (a_IV>0.80) 942 (53.3%)**. ⚠ **This is an a-priori, mechanistically-grounded PRIOR — NOT a validated filter:** its premise (that a_g modulates whether repression reaches protein) is near-definitional but **cannot be demonstrated in CPTAC at n=101** (see (b)). It is testable in a larger proteogenomic cohort. | **A/P** ((a) is a solid pooled measurement = S; (b) is a rigorous same-sample retraction = S; (c) is an ANNOTATION whose predictive claim is explicitly UNVALIDATED = P) | `output/learned/cptac_a_by_cn_direction.tsv`, `output/learned/{edge,program}_protein_consequence.tsv`, `output/learned/cptac_a_causal_iv.tsv` | **RETRACTS the "mechanistic clincher" of MH-106.** Verifies the premise for a protein-consequence filter, then declines to claim the filter is validated. it does not invent it.** | **S** (genome-wide posterior readouts from the canonical sampler; retention validated against an INDEPENDENT construct — per-family compartment loading — with a monotone dose-response and a pre-specified canonical case at p=9e-12) | `learned/readouts.py`; | MH-111 | **The composition confound, resolved at ARM → FAMILY → GENE: (a) the Wu-major fractions are a SIMPLEX, so the design's "hold Cancer-Epithelial out" is mathematically an ILLUSION (R²=1.000) — but EMPIRICALLY BENIGN (purity contributes 0.9% of the loss; CPE already absorbs it); (b) the confound is the MESENCHYMAL/VASCULAR axis — CAFs 10.7% · Endothelial 6.3% · Myeloid 5.6% · PVL 4.1% — while ADAPTIVE IMMUNE contributes exactly 0.0%; (c) it CONCENTRATES in ONE family (95% of a gene's loss); (d) but UNLIKE the proliferation and host confounds it does NOT localise to an arm — seed-family members are compartment-HOMOGENEOUS.** **(a) THE SIMPLEX.** `LEARNED_MODEL_METHODS §1` holds `Cancer Epithelial` OUT of the `deconv` block because *"conditioning on the compartment the target is expressed in over-controls the signal"*. **But the 9 Wu-major fractions sum to EXACTLY 1.0** (sd=0.0000, 1,059 participants) ⇒ **R²(Cancer-Epithelial ~ the 8 'held-out' lineages) = 1.0000** ⇒ conditioning on the 8 DETERMINES the malignant fraction. **The stated protection is an illusion.** **⭐ But the CONSEQUENCE is benign** — compositional reparametrisation (m = malignant fraction = the PURITY axis; w = the 8 renormalised to the non-malignant simplex = the purity-FREE stromal MIX) gives: **conditioning on PURITY alone → median retention 0.991 (0.9% loss); on the STROMAL MIX alone → 0.669 (33.1% loss); on BOTH → 0.651 (34.9%)**. ⇒ **the malignant fraction contributes ~1% — CPE (already in core C) has absorbed it. The over-control the design feared is NOT happening.** **(b) WHICH CELLS (add one lineage at a time, 2,302 couplings):** **CAFs 10.7% loss · Endothelial 6.3% · Myeloid 5.6% · PVL 4.1%** · Normal-Epithelial 1.5% · B-cells 0.6% · **T-cells 0.0% · Plasmablasts 0.0%**. ⇒ **the confound is the MESENCHYMAL/VASCULAR/MYELOID axis, NOT lymphocytes** — the four stromal lineages co-vary as ONE axis (Σ individual 28.8% ≈ joint 33.1%). **(c) GENE-LEVEL ALLOCATION (Q1).** Because the Shapley of a LINEAR aggregate is exactly β_f, allocation is ADDITIVE ⇒ `gene_retention = Σ_f β_f^deconv / Σ_f β_f` (a SHARE-WEIGHTED aggregate, not a naive gene-level re-fit). **1,502 genes: median 0.800 — cell_intrinsic 60.5% · partial 29.5% · composition_explained 10.0%.** **⭐ The loss is CONCENTRATED: the single worst family carries 95% (median) of a gene's composition loss** — the same architecture as MH-100 (proliferation, arm-concentration 0.85) and MH-101 (host). **(d) ARM → FAMILY (Q3) — an INFORMATIVE NEGATIVE.** Compartment identity is computed per ARM (corr(arm dose, lineage fraction)); **within-family SPREAD of compartment lock is median 0.0121** (90th pct 0.17) ⇒ **seed-family members are compartment-HOMOGENEOUS**, and all three arm summaries predict family retention identically (dose-dominant ρ=−0.132 · dose-weighted −0.134 · unweighted −0.129). ⇒ **going arm-first buys NOTHING for composition — the FAMILY is the right unit.** **This is a genuine architectural CONTRAST: the proliferation confound (MH-100) localises to ONE arm (miR-93-5p) and the host confound (MH-101) follows the DOSE-DOMINANT arm — because those are LOCUS properties (host gene, replication locus) on which same-seed members DIVERGE. Composition is an EXPRESSION property, on which co-expressed seed-family members AGREE.** Exceptions (top-5% divergent): miR-25/32/92/363/367 (spread 0.196), miR-30-5p (0.191), miR-146-5p (0.186). | **S** (simplex proven exactly; purity-vs-stroma separated by a compositional reparametrisation; per-lineage add-one screen on 2,302 couplings; gene allocation exact via Shapley additivity; arm homogeneity measured on 157 multi-arm families) | `output/learned/{composition_pinpoint,gene_composition_allocation,arm_compartment_loading,family_arm_dose_compartment,family_compartment_loading,readouts_edges}.tsv` | Completes the retention machinery of MH-110. **Corrects the stated rationale of `LEARNED_MODEL_METHODS §1` (the Cancer-Epithelial hold-out is an illusion) while VINDICATING the practice (purity contributes 0.9%).** Ledger MH-111. |
| MH-112 | **The `composition_explained` edges are 90.5% BULK-MIXING ARTIFACTS, not a hidden stromal network — so `retention` is a clean artifact filter, not a biology shredder. The 9.5% residue is a small, tractable candidate list of COMPARTMENT-INTRINSIC edges (47 both-stromal; top hit miR-21→RBPJ).** **THE QUESTION.** The model is cell-intrinsic by construction, so a `composition_explained` edge could be either (i) **bulk-mixing arithmetic** — the miRNA sits in ONE compartment and the target in the OTHER, so their correlation is compartment bookkeeping with no regulation (the miR-200/EMT class: epithelial miRNA, stromal targets); or (ii) a **REAL compartment-intrinsic edge** — miRNA AND target BOTH in the same compartment (e.g. a CAF miRNA repressing a CAF gene), in which case conditioning on that fraction DESTROYS real biology and we have been discarding a **stromal regulatory network**. **THE TEST (orientation):** compare sign(miRNA CAF-loading) vs sign(target CAF-loading) per coupling. **RESULT (4,456 couplings): of the 537 `composition_explained` edges, 486 (90.5%) are OPPOSITE-compartment (artifact) and only 51 (9.5%) SAME-compartment** — against a **cell_intrinsic baseline of 52.8% same-compartment** (≈ coin-flip, the correct null: when coupling is not compartment-driven, orientation is irrelevant). **Fisher OR = 0.09, p = 1.6e-87.** ⇒ **the "hidden stromal network" hypothesis is REFUTED at scale, and `retention` is VALIDATED as an artifact filter — it flags arithmetic, not biology.** **THE RESIDUE IS A DELIVERABLE:** 51 same-compartment composition-explained edges (**47 both-STROMAL**, 4 both-epithelial) = candidate **compartment-intrinsic** regulation the cell-intrinsic model structurally cannot see. **Top hit: RBPJ ← miR-21-5p/590-5p** (β=0.023, retention 0.34; miRNA CAF-loading **+0.214**, target CAF-loading **+0.267**) — **miR-21 is a canonical CAF/stromal miRNA and RBPJ is the Notch effector**, a biologically plausible stroma-intrinsic edge. | **S** (orientation test on 4,456 couplings against an internal cell-intrinsic null, Fisher p=1.6e-87; the candidate list is a hypothesis-generating residue, P) | `output/learned/edge_compartment_orientation.tsv`; `output/learned/{readouts_edges,family_compartment_loading}.tsv` | Validates the `retention` tag of MH-110/111 as an **artifact filter** (it is not discarding a stromal network). Yields a 47-edge candidate list of compartment-intrinsic regulation. Ledger MH-112. |
| MH-113 | **⛔ RETRACTION: "bulk protein is ~3× more compartment-exposed than bulk mRNA" is FALSE — it was a UNIT + COHORT artifact, not a layer effect.** The claim rested on comparing **per-FAMILY / TCGA / n=1041 / mRNA (14% composition_explained)** against **per-GENE-aggregate / CPTAC / n=101 / protein (40%)** — three things differed at once. **FAIR TEST (same cohort = CPTAC n=101, same unit = per family, same C; ONLY the outcome layer varies, 1,868 couplings):** **mRNA 38.5% composition_explained** (median retention 0.631) vs **protein 33.7%** (median retention 0.697); **Mann-Whitney (protein < mRNA) p = 0.986 ⇒ NO layer effect**, protein if anything slightly LESS exposed. **The real driver is COHORT SIZE:** TCGA per-family mRNA **14.0%** (n=1041) vs CPTAC per-family mRNA **38.5%** (n=101). At small n the β estimates are noisy and `retention = β_deconv/β_core` is an unstable RATIO (already QC'd: Spearman(\|β_core\|, \|retention\|) strongly negative) ⇒ extreme ratios ⇒ inflated composition_explained rate. **CONSEQUENCE: `retention` rates are NOT comparable across cohorts of different n.** Only within-cohort comparisons are valid. | **S** (same-cohort, same-unit, same-C test isolating the layer; n=1,868 couplings) | `output/learned/layer_retention_cptac.tsv` | **Voids the "proteogenomics warning" insight.** Adds a caveat to MH-110/111: retention rates are n-dependent and must not be compared across cohorts. |
| MH-114 | **The CPTAC bulk-PROTEIN validation of miRNA repression is ~57% COMPARTMENT ARITHMETIC — and this is PROVEN, not inferred: degree-preserving SHUFFLED non-edges reproduce the real edges' compartment gradient EXACTLY (−0.1290 vs −0.1272). Composition adjustment therefore removes ARTIFACT, not biology. The residual TRUE repression is real, compartment-blind, and ~40% of the advertised size.** **THE SETUP.** MH-107 fixed `cptac_validation`'s missing composition block; this closes the debt by RE-RUNNING it (both cohorts) — and finds a **second composition-blind lane**: `cptac_orphan_discovery` built `purity+cin` straight off the clinical frame, never calling `_covariates`, so the entire ORPHAN NOMINATION deliverable was screened with no composition block. **THE DAMAGE (measured):** prospective gene-level FDR-neg **34 → 3** (q<0.05); hallmark-level significantly-negative **9 → 0** (**EMT −0.330 → +0.162, sign flip**); **orphan candidates 594 → 23**; TCGA-105 had **0 before and after** ⇒ the gene-level protein validation rested entirely on the prospective cohort. **BUT NOT ABSENT — a POWER WALL:** the ex-hits remain significantly negative **as a set** (genes: adjusted mean −0.136 vs +0.009, MW **p=1.8e-08**; orphans: −0.146 vs +0.002, MW **p=4.2e-141**, 87% keep sign). At the adjusted \|ρ\|≈0.14, n=101 gives ~25% power uncorrected and ~0 after FDR. **Two independent lanes converge: 60% (HE genes) / 56% (orphans) of the apparent effect is arithmetic.** **⭐ THE DECISIVE TEST (the reason this is S, not P).** Compartment arithmetic is **blind to 3'UTR sites**; true repression is **blind to compartment**. So stratify a degree-preserving shuffled null (4,130 HE edges vs 10,032 non-edges) by orientation = sign(corr(miRNA,CAF))×sign(corr(protein,CAF)). **REAL: OPPOSITE −0.0735 / SAME +0.0537 ⇒ gradient −0.1272 (p=1.3e-96). SHUFFLED: OPPOSITE −0.0636 / SAME +0.0654 ⇒ gradient −0.1290 (p=8.5e-236).** **Edges that CANNOT repress — fake by construction — produce the SAME bulk anti-correlation as curated ones, purely by living in the opposite compartment.** Adjustment removes 90%/89% of the gradient (real/shuffled). ⇒ **the unadjusted bulk-protein signal IS compartment arithmetic.** **THE TRUE EFFECT = the compartment-BLIND real-minus-shuffled contrast:** −0.0099 (opposite) and −0.0117 (same) ⇒ **≈−0.011, the SAME in both orientations, as real repression must be** — and it SURVIVES adjustment at **≈−0.0065** (MW p=2.0e-03). **⚠ THE CROSS-COHORT TRAP (resolved):** adjustment appears to HURT reproducibility (prospective↔TCGA-105 Spearman +0.263 → +0.123, Fisher-z p=1.2e-03) — **because the confound is SHARED across two bulk breast cohorts, so the ARTIFACT REPLICATES.** ⇒ **"it reproduces in an independent cohort" is NOT evidence of biology when both cohorts carry the same confound.** **THE SIMPLEX (user-raised) is real but NOT the driver:** the 9 fractions sum to exactly 1.000000 and R²(Cancer-Epithelial ~ the 8 "held-out") = **1.000000 in BOTH cohorts** ⇒ tumour content IS implicitly conditioned; **but** a block with tumour content REMOVED (renormalised stromal MIX) reproduces the collapse (ZEB1 −0.199 vs −0.172) while the tumour-content axis ALONE does not (ZEB1 −0.446) ⇒ the stromal **MIX** is the driver, **not an over-control on purity**. Permuting the composition columns (same df) leaves ρ untouched (z=+14.3) ⇒ **not a df artifact**. | **S** (shuffled-null with a mechanism-based, non-circular ground truth; the artifact is reproduced in edges that cannot exist; permutation + simplex-decomposition controls; two independent lanes agree) | `output/cptac_validation_MH107/{,tcga105/}`, `output/cptac_validation/orphan_discovery/` (re-run), pre-fix preserved at `output/cptac_validation_PRE_MH107/`; `eval/cptac_validation._covariates`, `analyses/cptac/cptac_orphan_discovery` (both now composition-aware) | **Supersedes the protein leg of MH-34/35/36/39/40 and the orphan nominations.** ⛔ **RETRACTS my own intra-session "adjustment removes biology (over-control)" verdict** — it came from a MEAN-based shuffled null, and **the mean CANCELS a sign-symmetric artifact** (random pairs are equally likely same- or opposite-compartment) ⇒ **a shuffled null must be STRATIFIED BY THE CONFOUND'S OWN AXIS.** ⚠ **STALE: `presentation/F25_cptac.png` + `talk.qmd:315`** ("53/1143 genes FDR-neg; ZEB1 ρ=−0.55"; annotates **CALD1/MMP2 — CAF markers**) and the "108 orphans triple-validated" claim (`buffa_validation`). | MH-115 | ⚠⚠ **RESTATED BY MH-127 — THE HEADLINE AS WRITTEN IS A CLAIM ABOUT *FITTING*, NOT CURATION.** The benchmark below compared a **FITTED** β against **UNFITTED** abundance. MH-127 supplied the missing arm — a **fitted MATCHED-FAKE** (abundance-matched, site-free, non-family, non-cluster, \|r\|<0.30) regulator set — and it **beats real unfitted abundance** (TCGA OOF Δ=−0.0100 p=0.054 all genes; **Δ=−0.0357 p=3.6e-04** on multi-regulator genes) while **reaching 78.9–91.3% of the real fitted model** (real-vs-fake Wilcoxon **p=0.211/0.577, n.s.**). **CORRECTED WORDING:** *the learned β out-predicts the pressure heuristic and raw abundance — but so does a matched IMPOSSIBLE regulator set once it is FITTED; the advantage over abundance is a FITTING effect. | MH-123 | **⭐⭐ THE PER-EDGE NULL IS MISCALIBRATED — the theoretical t-null is wrong by 3–4× in SCALE, so per-edge miRNA→target discovery in TCGA bulk is essentially impossible at n=1041, and the HE signal is a SET-LEVEL DISTRIBUTIONAL SHIFT, not per-edge significance.** **THE DIAGNOSIS.** A SITE-FREE class — (arm,gene) pairs with NO TargetScan context++ site AND no scanMiR duplex (repression > −0.5), i.e. pairs that **cannot repress by any modelled mechanism** but carry the SAME population structure. Measured (TCGA, n=1041, 60,000 site-free pairs): under the theoretical t-null **35.3% of them are "FDR-significant" (q<0.05, ρ<0) under core C and 25.0% under composition-adjusted C** — a 25–35% false-positive rate at a nominal 5% FDR. **THE FITTED NULL** (per arm-abundance quintile, robust median / 1.4826·MAD): theoretical would be μ=0, **sd=0.0311**; measured site-free null is **μ₀ = −0.048…−0.021, sd₀ = 0.105…0.132 (core C)** and **μ₀ = −0.027…−0.011, sd₀ = 0.083…0.097 (composition-adjusted)** ⇒ **the real null is 3–4× WIDER than the t-distribution assumes AND its mean is NEGATIVE** (a global miRNA↔mRNA anti-correlation from shared population structure). Composition adjustment shrinks both shift and width but does **NOT** close the gap ⇒ the confound is only partly compartmental. **RECALIBRATED COUNTS** (composition-adjusted C, Efron empirical null, BH q<0.05): **HE 1,313 (31.3%) → 1 (0.0%)** · ORPHAN 3,891 (83.5%) → 200 (4.3%) · site-free 14,971 (25.0%) → 0 (calibrated by construction). **⭐⭐ THE RECONCILIATION — THIS DOES *NOT* SAY THE EDGES ARE FAKE (both are true):** HE edges ARE genuinely enriched — abundance-matched (log2RPM>4) vs the site-free class **OR 1.41, Fisher p=2.5e-13, and it SURVIVES composition adjustment (OR 1.43 core → 1.41 deconv ⇒ THE CURATED SIGNAL WAS NEVER COMPARTMENT ARITHMETIC)** — but a typical HE edge has **ρ≈−0.15 against σ₀=0.09 ⇒ z≈−1.4, p≈0.08**: real, but nowhere near surviving FDR across 4,000 tests. The ORPHAN's 200 survivors are CIRCULAR SELECTION showing through (nominated FOR extreme ρ). **⭐ SEQUENCE CANDIDACY IS MOSTLY COMPARTMENT ARITHMETIC, CURATION IS NOT — composition adjustment does not wash everything out, it SEPARATES:** predicted-site (CAND) vs site-free (FAKE) **OR 1.36 (p=7e-24) → 1.11 (p=8e-04)** under composition, while **HE vs FAKE is UNCHANGED (1.43→1.41)** and HE vs CAND goes from n.s. (OR 1.05, p=0.12) to **significant (OR 1.27, p=5e-08)**. **⚠ THE FIRST IMPLEMENTATION WAS BROKEN AND THE GIVEAWAY WAS HE→0:** counting EXCEEDANCES against a 12k-per-bin null is RESOLUTION-LIMITED (min p = 1/N ≈ 8e-5), so BH across 4,194 tests (needing p<1.2e-5) can NEVER fire; fixed with the Efron form (FIT the null's location+scale, score against N(μ₀,σ₀), continuous p). **⇒ CONSEQUENCE (load-bearing): every per-edge FDR count in this subproject — incl. (p=0.34), all else n.s. — own-retaining tumours are NOT clinically distinct. THE TEST (board §Y / METHODS §4): per HE gene (1,387; ~1000 shared TCGA patients) fit `r ~ M + ago + prolif + M:ago + M:prolif` and test the **M:ago** coefficient, where `r=−resid(Y|C)` (repression readout), `M=Xhe_z @ β_card` (canonical de-confounded budget, == `discovery.py` he_agg **verbatim**), `ago=ago_capacity_z` (co-limited AGO1-4 ∧ TNRC6, `ago_gate.py`). **PRE-REGISTERED (direction positive = RISC amplifies repression); base coupling corr(M,r)=+0.10 / 72% positive = M is a live predictor (positive control).** **RESULT — UNDETECTED:** the ago-SPECIFIC interaction (ago ⊥ confounder block: composition+prolif+purity, **R²(ago~C)=0.395** ⇒ 40% of ago IS the confounders) is **M:ago_perp = +0.00656, frac-positive 56.0%**, calibrated against the **SHUFFLED-AGO null (the only honest arbiter — the per-gene t's are NOT independent: shared patients + shared ago): null +0.00025±0.00390 ⇒ z=+1.62, perm p=0.116** (frac z=+1.10). **⛔ THE NAIVE STOUFFER Z=+7.8 (p=5.6e-15) IS INVALID PSEUDO-REPLICATION** — the null SE 0.0039 shows ~1,387 genes carry ≈14 tests' independent information. **POWER is the teeth: MDE@80% ≈ 0.0109 = 1.7× the observed effect ⇒ ~40% power at the observed +1.6σ trend** — a sub-significant positive trend the design cannot resolve at n≈1000, NOT proven-zero. **OVER-CONTROL — checked, and the SAFE direction (corrected 2026-07-18):** on identical data the RAW-ago interaction is **+0.00118** (≈0) vs ⊥C **+0.00656** — residualisation does NOT strip signal; ago's 40% proliferation-correlated component carries the *opposite* (negative) slope-modification (consistent with **M:prolif Stouffer −19**, a working positive control for the interaction machinery), so ⊥C *un-masks* the weak ago-specific trend rather than manufacturing it; either way UNDETECTED. ⚠ **Only M:prolif is carried as an explicit slope-modifier interaction; M:purity/M:composition are not** (conservative-safe for a null — would shrink, not inflate). This does NOT mean coupling is noise — it means **TCGA breast coupling measures REALIZED DOMINANT regulation across breast patients (observational, variance-driven, composition/prolif-adjusted, conservative null), a HARDER & DIFFERENT question than edge EXISTENCE (McGeary functional capacity, HeLa/HEK).** Coupling is high-SPECIFICITY (2.9–5× null) / low-SENSITIVITY (~13% — the fraction of real edges that are dominant variance-drivers in bulk breast; the other 87% are real but observationally sub-dominant / low-variance / bulk-averaged). ⇒ **My error was USING coupling as an existence arbiter** — the "not missing breast edges" verdict OVER-CLAIMED (13%-sensitive gate ⇒ ~8–10 detected could be ~5–8× more undetected). The other 607 arms (82%, 47% of edges) = the low-expression high-numbered tail the field never transfects/CLIPs = UNVALIDATED (extrapolated, not measured). So precision/recall/coupling conclusions hold for the canonical core, NOT the tail — a structural canonical-miRNA-bias limit, not a fixable search gap.** **⭐ BREAST CHIMERIC (GSE263552) as an ORTHOGONAL breast validator (binding, not coupling) — the flagged follow-up, done:** HE edges are **27× enriched** for a breast chimeric duplex (4.9% vs 0.2% null) ⇒ a STRONG breast-specific arbiter (unlike TCGA coupling which was reality-independent), though sparse (miR-eCLIP under-samples). **DISCOVERY: 740 breast-binding candidate edges** (arm→Hallmark, direct duplex, not HE), top = let-7b→MYC (839 reads), miR-16→SPARC, miR-17/20a→TXNIP, miR-19b→SERPINE1, miR-23a→HLA-B; **6 double-confirmed** (breast binding + McGeary functional): let-7a→{THBS1,TRIB1,POU2F1,SLC20A1,SKIL}, miR-155→ATP6V1G1. | MH-167 | **⭐ THE DECOY IS NOT MATCHED IN FAMILY STRUCTURE — AND IT DOES NOT MATTER: a PASSING CONTROL that BOUNDS the contamination of the −0.0129 gap at ≤~0.0025 ρ.** User-asked (2026-08-01). `eval/decoy_bench.py` matches **ARMS** (signed loadings + dose + variance, Hungarian 1:1) and collapses each side to seed families only **AFTERWARD**, so two properties of the design the estimator actually sees were never in the cost function — and `_one` emitted only `Xr.shape[1]` (the REAL width), making the asymmetry **unmeasurable from the artifact**. **BOTH ASYMMETRIES ARE REAL.** Design **WIDTH**: real **3.096** vs fake **3.510** families (Δ **+0.414**, Wilcoxon **p=4.6e-42**) — curation genuinely **BUNDLES same-seed mates** (**1.087** arms/family real vs **1.004** fake: fakes essentially never share a family) — but **81.8% of genes are EQUAL-width** and the mismatch is a **17.8% wide-design TAIL** (+1.82 families at real `n_fam≥5`). Within-design **COLLINEARITY**: real **0.1663** vs fake **0.1547** (Δ **−0.0116**, **p=6.7e-03**), the real design more collinear, as co-transcribed cluster mates predict. **⭐ BUT NEITHER MOVES THE GAP:** `b(Δn_fam) = −0.00090 (p=0.722)` ⇒ contamination **−0.0004 [−0.0025, +0.0017]**; `b(Δcollin) = −0.02367 (p=0.618)` ⇒ **+0.0003 [−0.0008, +0.0014]**; **Δdose remains the ONLY design axis with a detectable effect** (`b=+0.01061, p=0.020`) — and that is already removed post hoc, which IS MH-139's correction. ⇒ **the unmatched family structure contaminates the −0.0129 by ≤~0.0025 ρ at the CI edge (~19% of it), point estimate ~3%.** ⚠⚠ **MY PRE-REGISTERED PREDICTION WAS WRONG, AND IS RECORDED AS SUCH (axiom 1a).** Predicted: the asymmetries **DEFLATE** the gap (a wider, better-conditioned fake fits better) ⇒ "−0.0129 is conservative". Measured: the effect is **UNDETECTED, not directional**. **⛔ Do NOT carry a "conservative" framing from this control** — the defensible output is a **BOUND on contamination**, nothing more. **This is a control that PASSES, which this axis is short on** (MH-136 is the only other one). ⚠ **It is a CONTROL, not a fix:** had an asymmetry moved the gap, the answer would have been to MATCH on it, never to reweight the headline. Made permanent: `n_fam_fake` / `collin_real` / `collin_fake` are now emitted by `decoy_bench._one` (purely additive — the re-run reproduced every gap figure bit-identically), and the control is reproducible at `eval/decoy_design_match.py`. | P | `output/learned/decoy_design_match.tsv`, `output/learned/decoy_bench.tsv`, `eval/decoy_design_match.py` | Companion to MH-137/139. Since β is broadcast equally within a seed family this covers every one-family gene by construction. **MEASURED: 540 constant-β genes, max\|gain\| = 0.000e+00 — EXACT.** A non-zero value there would have been an implementation bug, not a finding; this is the strongest kind of control the arc can have and it passes bit-exactly. **RESULT (TCGA, n≈1077, 836 variable-β genes): mean gain +0.0612, median +0.0366, HELPS IN 83%, Wilcoxon p=9.7e-111.** **MECHANISM — BOTH axes contribute and they are SUPPRESSORS of each other:** marginal spearman(gain, `n_fam`) **+0.599**, (gain, `beta_conc`) **+0.235**, (gain, `n_arm`) **+0.614**; **PARTIALS BOTH SURVIVE and GROW — `beta_conc | n_fam` = +0.321 (p=2e-21), `n_fam | beta_conc` = +0.587 (p=1.6e-78)**. This characterises WHERE the weighting acts, not whether it is correct; the benchmark for that is a fitted fake (`eval/decoy_bench`). | S | `learned/weight_gain.py`; `output/learned/weight_gain_profile.tsv` | Explains MH-175's small transfer margin: the weighting is real but concentrated in wide, uneven designs, and 43% of the universe is constant-β where it CANNOT act. |
